namespace = earn_recognition_events

earn_recognition_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	title = earn_recognition_events.1.t
	desc = earn_recognition_events.1.d
	flavor = earn_recognition_events.1.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_signed_contract"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		# Triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				country_rank >= rank_value:great_power
				relations:root >= relations_threshold:amicable
			}
			save_scope_as = treaty_partner_country_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = earn_recognition_events.1.a
		default_option = yes
		set_country_type = recognized
		change_relations = { country = scope:treaty_partner_country_scope value = 30 }
		create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
			type = catalyst_event_positive
			target = scope:treaty_partner_country_scope
		}
	}
}

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		#Fired by on_action
	}

	immediate = {

	}

	option = {
		name = gg_afghanistan.2.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 75
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = unlock_additional_proposals_for_afghanistan_tt
			set_variable = afghanistan_rejection
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = proposed_reprisals_for_afghanistan

			change_relations = {
				country = c:AFG
				value = -30
			}
		}
		reset_afghan_treaty = yes
	}
}

gg_afghanistan.3 = { #Afghanistan petitions for assistance
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = gg_afghanistan.3.t
	desc = gg_afghanistan.3.d
	flavor = gg_afghanistan.3.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_redcoats"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		#Fired by Button
	}

	immediate = {

	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		exists = c:AFG
	}

	option = {
		name = gg_afghanistan.3.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 75
		}
		trigger = {
			can_create_treaty = {
				first_country = root
				second_country = scope:afghan_nation_scope
				is_draft = no
				binding_period = { years = 2 }
				articles_to_create = {
					{
						article = military_assistance
						source_country = root
						target_country = scope:afghan_nation_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = committed_to_afghanistan_assistance
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:RUS ?= this
			}
			scope:afghan_nation_scope = {
				set_variable = { name = AFG_appealed_to_RUS days = very_long_modifier_time }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:GBR ?= this
			}
			scope:afghan_nation_scope = {
				set_variable = { name = AFG_appealed_to_GBR days = very_long_modifier_time }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					scope:afghan_nation_scope = {
						owes_obligation_to = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			create_treaty = {
				first_country = root
				second_country = scope:afghan_nation_scope

				is_draft = no
				binding_period = { years = 2 }

				articles_to_create = {
					{
						article = military_assistance
						source_country = root
						target_country = scope:afghan_nation_scope
					}
				}
			}
			scope:afghan_nation_scope = {
				set_owes_obligation_to = {
					country = ROOT
					setting = yes
				}
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_afghan_military_training_costs
			days = short_modifier_time
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_small
		}
		scope:afghan_nation_scope = {
			post_notification = afghanistan_assistance_request_accepted
			remove_variable = pending_aid_request
		}
		create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
			type = catalyst_event_positive
			target = scope:afghan_nation_scope
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:RUS ?= this
			}
			scope:afghan_nation_scope = {
				create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
					type = catalyst_event_negative
					target = c:GBR
				}
			}
			root.power_bloc = {
				add_leverage = { target = scope:afghan_nation_scope value = 25 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				c:GBR ?= this
			}
			scope:afghan_nation_scope = {
				create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
					type = catalyst_event_negative
					target = c:RUS
				}
			}
			root.power_bloc = {
				add_leverage = { target = scope:afghan_nation_scope value = 25 }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gg_afghanistan.3.b
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
		scope:afghan_nation_scope = {
			post_notification = afghanistan_assistance_request_rejected
			remove_variable = pending_aid_request
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_player = yes
				c:GBR ?= this
			}
			scope:afghan_nation_scope = {
				set_variable = player_GBR_denied_aid_request
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_player = yes
				c:RUS ?= this
			}
			scope:afghan_nation_scope = {
				set_variable = player_RUS_denied_aid_request
			}
		}
	}
}

gg_afghanistan.4 = { #Afghanistan unifies
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = gg_afghanistan.4.t
	desc = gg_afghanistan.4.d
	flavor = gg_afghanistan.4.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_central_asian_city"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		#Fired by Unification
	}

	immediate = {

	}

	option = {
		name = gg_afghanistan.4.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 75
		}
		ordered_country = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
					is_homeland_of_country_cultures = ROOT
				}
			}
			order_by = gdp
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			ROOT = {
				create_incident = {
					country = PREV
					value = 15
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_state_region = {
					any_scope_state = {
						is_homeland_of_country_cultures = ROOT
						NOT = { owner = ROOT }
						NOT = { has_claim_by = ROOT }
					}
				}
			}
			every_state_region = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_state = {
						is_homeland_of_country_cultures = ROOT
						NOT = { owner = ROOT }
						NOT = { has_claim_by = ROOT }
					}
				}
				add_claim = ROOT
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_loyalists = {
				value = 0.05
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = unification_prestige
			days = very_long_modifier_time
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gg_afghanistan.4.b
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = unification_prestige
			days = very_long_modifier_time
			multiplier = 2
		}
	}
}

gg_afghanistan.7 = { # The Man Who Would Be King
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = gg_afghanistan.7.t
	desc = gg_afghanistan.7.d
	flavor = gg_afghanistan.7.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_persian_court"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = ep1_content
		c:KAF ?= this
		any_neighbouring_state = {
			owner = {
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = root
				}
				any_scope_character = {
					has_template = KAB_josiah_harlan
				}
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = man_who_would_be_king_var
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = {
			exists = scope:the_man_who_would_be_king_scope
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = man_who_would_be_king_var
		random_neighbouring_state = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					NOT = {
						has_war_with = root
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = KAB_josiah_harlan
					}
				}
			}
			owner = {
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = KAB_josiah_harlan
					}
					save_scope_as = the_man_who_would_be_king_scope
				}
				save_scope_as = the_man_who_would_be_king_origin_scope
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gg_afghanistan.7.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			value = small_radicals
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gg_afghanistan.7.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
		scope:the_man_who_would_be_king_scope = {
			transfer_character = root
			set_character_as_ruler = yes
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = josiah_harlan_guns_modifier
			days = long_modifier_time
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = medium_radicals
		}
	}
}
﻿namespace = caucasuswar

#These events have been re-balanced to swap to single-fire on yearly pulse. They can possibly repeat if the JE remains longer than 20 years.
caucasuswar.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:invasion_base_state

	title = caucasuswar.1.t
	desc = caucasuswar.1.d
	flavor = caucasuswar.1.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/unspecific_sick_in_hospital.bk2"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = short_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			exists = c:CIR
			exists = c:CHC
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		root = {
			save_scope_as = russia_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_DAGESTAN
					state_region = s:STATE_KUBAN
					state_region = s:STATE_NORTH_CAUCASUS
				}
				is_split_state = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = invasion_base_state
		}
		scope:invasion_base_state = {
			this.state_region = {
				random_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							c:RUS ?= owner
						}
						OR = {
							c:CHC ?= owner
							c:CIR ?= owner
						}
					}
					owner = {
						save_scope_as = adversary_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar.1.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:invasion_base_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_failed_incursion
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
			this.state_region = {
				add_devastation = 15
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				culture = cu:circassian
			}
		}
		scope:adversary_scope = {
			post_notification = caucasus_russian_failed_attack_notification
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar.1.b
		scope:invasion_base_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_failed_incursion
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
			this.state_region = {
				add_devastation = 5
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = officers
				culture = cu:russian
			}
			this.region = {
				random_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						owner = c:RUS
						any_scope_building = {
							is_building_type = building_barrack
						}
					}
					add_radicals_in_state = {
						value = small_radicals
						pop_type = officers
						culture = cu:russian
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_failed_to_cover_incursion
				months = 13
			}
		}
		scope:adversary_scope = {
			post_notification = caucasus_russian_failed_attack_notification
		}
	}
}

					months = 13
				}
			}
		}
		scope:invasion_state = {
			state_region = {
				add_devastation = 5
			}
		}
		scope:adversary_scope = {
			post_notification = caucasus_russian_village_burned_notification
		}
	}
}

caucasuswar.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:invasion_state

	title = caucasuswar.4.t
	desc = caucasuswar.4.d
	flavor = caucasuswar.4.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/ep1_ca_mountain_pass_civilian.bk2"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = short_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			c:CIR ?= {
				NOT = {
					has_truce_with = c:RUS
				}
			}
			c:CHC ?= {
				NOT = {
					has_truce_with = c:RUS
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		root = {
			save_scope_as = russia_scope
		}
		random_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					c:CHC ?= owner
					c:CIR ?= owner
				}
				is_split_state = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = invasion_state
		}
		scope:invasion_state = {
			owner = {
				save_scope_as = adversary_scope
			}
		}
		scope:adversary_scope = {
			random_primary_culture = {
				save_scope_as = defector_culture
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar.4.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:adversary_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_betrayal_of_tribe
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:adversary_scope = {
			post_notification = caucasus_tribe_defection_notification
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar.4.b
		scope:adversary_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_betrayal_of_tribe
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				any_neighbouring_state = {
					this = scope:invasion_state
				}
			}
			add_culture_standard_of_living_modifier = {
				culture = scope:defector_culture
				multiplier = 2
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = officers
				culture = cu:russian
			}
		}
		scope:adversary_scope = {
			post_notification = caucasus_tribe_defection_notification
		}
	}
}

caucasuswar.5 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = caucasuswar.5.t
	desc = caucasuswar.5.d
	flavor = caucasuswar.5.f

	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/ep1_cossacks.bk2"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		c:RUS ?= this
		NOT = {
			has_variable = milyutin_memorandum
		}
		exists = c:CIR
		NOT = {
			has_truce_with = c:CIR
		}
		OR = {
			cultural_acceptance_base = { target = cu:circassian value < acceptance_status_4 }
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_state_religion
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			state_region = s:STATE_KUBAN
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		c:CIR ?= {
			save_scope_as = adversary_scope
		}
		create_character = {
			template = RUS_dmitry_milyutin
			on_created = {
				set_variable = milyutin_variable
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = milyutin_variable
			}
			save_scope_as = milyutin_scope
		}
		cu:circassian = {
			save_scope_as = circassian_culture
		}
		set_variable = milyutin_memorandum
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar.5.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = milyutin_available_tt
		}
		custom_tooltip = will_lead_to_expulsions_tt
		set_variable = milyutin_memorandum_accepted
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_KUBAN
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_milyutin_memorandum
				days = long_modifier_time
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = very_large_radicals
				culture = cu:circassian
			}
		}
		scope:milyutin_scope = {
			add_trait = war_criminal
		}
		scope:adversary_scope = {
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_negative
				target = root
			}
		}
		c:TUR ?= {
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_negative
				target = root
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar.5.b
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = milyutin_available_tt
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_refused_milyutin_memorandum
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = very_large_radicals
			pop_type = officers
			culture = cu:russian
		}
		scope:milyutin_scope = {
			add_trait = experienced_colonial_administrator
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

caucasuswar.6 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:invasion_base_state

	title = caucasuswar.6.t
	desc = caucasuswar.6.d
	flavor = caucasuswar.6.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/ep1_ca_mountain_pass_military.bk2"
	}

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = short_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		exists = c:CHC
	}

	immediate = {
		root = {
			save_scope_as = russia_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_KUBAN
					state_region = s:STATE_NORTH_CAUCASUS
				}
				is_split_state = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = invasion_base_state
		}
		c:CHC ?= {
			save_scope_as = adversary_scope
			ruler = {
				save_scope_as = shamil_scope
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar.6.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:invasion_base_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_soldiers_deserted
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = soldiers
			}
			add_culture_standard_of_living_modifier = {
				culture = cu:north_caucasian
				multiplier = -2
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		c:CHC ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_receiving_russian_deserters
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:adversary_scope = {
			post_notification = caucasus_russian_soldiers_defection_notification
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar.6.b
		scope:invasion_base_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_soldiers_deserted
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		c:CHC ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_receiving_russian_deserters
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_rampant_desertions
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:adversary_scope = {
			post_notification = caucasus_russian_soldiers_defection_notification
		}
	}
}

caucasuswar.7 = { #Parliament of Independence
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = caucasuswar.7.t
	desc = caucasuswar.7.d
	flavor = caucasuswar.7.f

	dlc = dlc010

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/unspecific_ruler_speaking_to_people.bk2"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_truce_with = c:RUS
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		c:RUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = russia_scope
		}
		capital = {
			save_scope_as = capital_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar.7.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_law = law_type:law_professional_army
				}
			}
			activate_law = law_type:law_professional_army
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:north_caucasian
				}
			}
			add_primary_culture = cu:north_caucasian
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_circassian_last_stand
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		create_character = {
			# Qerandiqo Berzeg
			first_name = Qerandiqo
			last_name = Berzeg
			birth_date = 1804.6.1 #Only year known
			ruler = yes
			hq = region_caucasus
			is_general = yes
			commander_rank = commander_rank_3
			interest_group = ig_landowners
			ideology = ideology_moderate
			traits = {
				persistent
				brave
				forest_commander
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar.7.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:north_caucasian
				}
			}
			add_primary_culture = cu:north_caucasian
		}
		create_character = {
			# Qerandiqo Berzeg
			first_name = Qerandiqo
			last_name = Berzeg
			birth_date = 1804.6.1 #Only year known
			ruler = yes
			hq = region_caucasus
			is_general = yes
			commander_rank = commander_rank_3
			interest_group = ig_landowners
			ideology = ideology_moderate
			traits = {
				persistent
				brave
				forest_commander
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_circassian_diplomacy
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
				has_interest_marker_in_region = region_caucasus
				NOT = {
					c:RUS ?= THIS
				}
			}
			trigger_event = { id = caucasuswar.8 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar.7.c
		trigger = {
			ruler.interest_group = {
				is_interest_group_type = ig_devout
			}
			exists = c:CHC
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_treaty = {
						binds = c:CHC
						any_scope_article = {
							has_type = alliance
						}
					}
				}
			}
			create_treaty = {
				first_country = root
				second_country = c:CHC

				is_draft = no
				binding_period = { years = 5 }

				articles_to_create = {
					{
						article = alliance
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_circassian_joint_struggle
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		c:CHC ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_circassian_joint_struggle
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		c:TUR ?= {
			trigger_event = { id = caucasuswar.8 }
		}
	}
}

caucasuswar.10 = { #Teofil Lapinski's volunteers
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = caucasuswar.10.t
	desc = caucasuswar.10.d
	flavor = caucasuswar.10.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/ep1_redcoats.bk2"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_truce_with = c:RUS
		}
		game_date > 1855.1.1
		NOT = {
			has_variable = lapinski_variable
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		cu:polish = {
			save_scope_as = polish_culture
		}
		cu:hungarian = {
			save_scope_as = hungarian_culture
		}
		ordered_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_WEST_GALICIA
			}
			order_by = state_population
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			save_scope_as = galicia_scope
			owner = {
				save_scope_as = austria_scope
			}
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = russia_scope
		}
		set_variable = lapinski_variable
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar.10.a
		default_option = yes
		create_character = {
			is_general = yes
			first_name = Teofil
			last_name = Lapinski
			culture = cu:polish
			historical = yes
			birth_date = 1827.12.19
			hq = region_caucasus
			ideology = ideology_radical
			interest_group = ig_armed_forces
			commander_rank = commander_rank_1
			traits = {
				persistent
				explorer
				celebrity_commander
			}
		}
		capital = {
			random_scope_building = {
				limit = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_lapinski_expedition
					days = short_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar.10.b
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_lapinski_training
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
}

namespace = caucasuswar_end

caucasuswar_end.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = caucasuswar_end.1.t
	desc = caucasuswar_end.1.d
	flavor = caucasuswar_end.1.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/ep1_cossacks.bk2"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			exists = c:CIR
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = circassia_defeated
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = circassia_defeated
		random_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_KUBAN
			}
			save_scope_as = kuban_state
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar_end.1.a
		default_option = yes
		trigger = {
			scope:kuban_state = {
				has_modifier = modifier_milyutin_memorandum
			}
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			value = medium_radicals
			culture = cu:russian
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			value = medium_radicals
			culture = cu:ukrainian
		}
		scope:kuban_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_settlers_privileges
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:kuban_state = {
				remove_modifier = caucasus_war_attacker
			}
		}
		scope:kuban_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_caucasian_war_sol
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
			state_region = {
				add_homeland = cu:russian
				add_homeland = cu:ukrainian
			}
		}
		if = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = milyutin_memorandum_accepted
				NOT = {
					has_journal_entry = je_circassian_expulsions
				}
				cultural_acceptance_base = { target = cu:circassian value < acceptance_status_4 }
            }
            add_journal_entry = { type = je_circassian_expulsions }
        }
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar_end.1.b
		trigger = {
			scope:kuban_state = {
				NOT = {
					has_modifier = modifier_milyutin_memorandum
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:kuban_state = {
				remove_modifier = caucasus_war_attacker
			}
		}
		scope:kuban_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_caucasian_war_sol
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
			state_region = {
				add_homeland = cu:russian
				add_homeland = cu:ukrainian
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = modifier_caucasian_war_sol
			}
			remove_modifier = modifier_caucasian_war_sol
		}
		remove_modifier = caucasus_war_defender_circassia
		add_modifier = modifier_honour_and_life_military
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar_end.2.c
		trigger = {
			any_country = {
				has_variable = circassia_recognizer
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = modifier_caucasian_war_sol
			}
			remove_modifier = modifier_caucasian_war_sol
		}
		set_country_type = recognized
		remove_modifier = caucasus_war_defender_circassia
		scope:circassia_recognizer_scope = {
			change_relations = {
				country = c:CIR
				value = 25
			}
		}
	}
}


caucasuswar_end.3 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = caucasuswar_end.3.t
	desc = caucasuswar_end.3.d
	flavor = caucasuswar_end.3.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/ep1_ca_mountain_pass_military.bk2"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			exists = c:CHC
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = imamate_defeated
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = imamate_defeated
		c:RUS ?= {
			ruler = {
				save_scope_as = rus_ruler_scope
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_DAGESTAN
				}
			}
			random_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = s:STATE_DAGESTAN
				}
				save_scope_as = dagestan_state
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_NORTH_CAUCASUS
				}
			}
			random_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = s:STATE_NORTH_CAUCASUS
				}
				save_scope_as = north_caucasus_state
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar_end.3.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:dagestan_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_legacy_of_shamil
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
		scope:north_caucasus_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_legacy_of_shamil
			}
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:dagestan_state = {
				remove_modifier = caucasus_war_attacker
			}
			scope:north_caucasus_state = {
				remove_modifier = caucasus_war_attacker
			}
		}
		scope:dagestan_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_caucasian_war_sol
				days = short_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		scope:north_caucasus_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_caucasian_war_sol
				days = short_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasuswar_end.3.b
		scope:dagestan_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = very_large_radicals
				culture = cu:north_caucasian
			}
		}
		scope:north_caucasus_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = very_large_radicals
				culture = cu:north_caucasian
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:dagestan_state = {
				remove_modifier = caucasus_war_attacker
			}
			scope:north_caucasus_state = {
				remove_modifier = caucasus_war_attacker
			}
		}
		scope:dagestan_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_caucasian_war_sol
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		scope:north_caucasus_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_caucasian_war_sol
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

namespace = caucasus_expulsions

caucasus_expulsions.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:circassian_expulsion_state

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"
	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_ca_mountain_pass_civilian"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	title = caucasus_expulsions.1.t
	desc = caucasus_expulsions.1.d
	flavor = caucasus_expulsions.1.f

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_variable = circassian_expulsion_stall }
		has_journal_entry = je_circassian_expulsions
		any_scope_state = {
			region = sr:region_caucasus
			any_scope_pop = {
				culture = cu:circassian
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = circassian_events_hidden
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_caucasus
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:circassian
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = circassian_expulsion_state
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
					OR = {
						state_region = s:STATE_EASTERN_THRACE
						state_region = s:STATE_KASTAMONU
						state_region = s:STATE_TRABZON
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = expulsion_destination_country
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						state_region = s:STATE_EASTERN_THRACE
						state_region = s:STATE_KASTAMONU
						state_region = s:STATE_TRABZON
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = expulsion_destination_state
			}
		}
		cu:circassian = {
			save_scope_as = circassian_culture_scope
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = circassian_expulsion_stall
			months = 3
		}
		ROOT = {
			save_scope_as = russia_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasus_expulsions.1.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:circassian_expulsion_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:expulsion_destination_country = {
						has_variable = refused_circassian_refugees
					}
				}
				kill_population_percent_in_state = {
					percent = 0.06
					culture = cu:circassian
				}
			}
			else = {
				kill_population_percent_in_state = {
					percent = 0.04
					culture = cu:circassian
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = three_pops_deported_tt
				random_scope_pop = {
					limit = {
						culture = cu:circassian
						strata = lower
					}
					move_pop = scope:expulsion_destination_state
				}
				random_scope_pop = {
					limit = {
						culture = cu:circassian
						strata = middle
					}
					move_pop = scope:expulsion_destination_state
				}
				random_scope_pop = {
					limit = {
						culture = cu:circassian
						strata = upper
					}
					move_pop = scope:expulsion_destination_state
				}
			}
		}
		scope:expulsion_destination_country = {
			post_notification = ottoman_circassian_refugees_arrived
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasus_expulsions.1.b
		scope:circassian_expulsion_state = {
			kill_population_percent_in_state = {
				percent = 0.01
				culture = cu:circassian
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = three_pops_deported_tt
				random_scope_pop = {
					limit = {
						culture = cu:circassian
						OR = {
							strata = lower
							strata = middle
						}
					}
					move_pop = scope:expulsion_destination_state
				}
				random_scope_pop = {
					limit = {
						culture = cu:circassian
						OR = {
							strata = middle
							strata = upper
						}
					}
					move_pop = scope:expulsion_destination_state
				}
			}
		}
		scope:expulsion_destination_country = {
			post_notification = ottoman_circassian_refugees_arrived
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = caucasus_expulsions.1.c
		set_variable = circassian_events_hidden
		custom_tooltip = hide_deportation_events_tt
		scope:circassian_expulsion_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:expulsion_destination_country = {
						has_variable = refused_circassian_refugees
					}
				}
				kill_population_percent_in_state = {
					percent = 0.06
					culture = cu:circassian
				}
			}
			else = {
				kill_population_percent_in_state = {
					percent = 0.04
					culture = cu:circassian
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = three_pops_deported_tt
				random_scope_pop = {
					limit = {
						culture = cu:circassian
						strata = lower
					}
					move_pop = scope:expulsion_destination_state
				}
				random_scope_pop = {
					limit = {
						culture = cu:circassian
						strata = middle
					}
					move_pop = scope:expulsion_destination_state
				}
				random_scope_pop = {
					limit = {
						culture = cu:circassian
						strata = upper
					}
					move_pop = scope:expulsion_destination_state
				}
			}
		}
		scope:expulsion_destination_country = {
			post_notification = ottoman_circassian_refugees_arrived
		}
	}
}

	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"

		}

		name = caucasus_expulsions.2.a

				value = 10
			}

			add_tradition_trait = tradition_rumelian
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 4
		}
	}

	option = { # Reject the proposal
		name = caucasus_expulsions.2.b
		set_variable = refused_circassian_refugees
		custom_tooltip = circassian_refugees_deportations_tt
		custom_tooltip = circassian_refugees_warning_tt

			change_relations = {
				country = ROOT
				value = -5
			}
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_negative
				target = root
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

﻿namespace = gg_core

gg_core.1 = { # Great Game victory
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = gg_core.1.t
	desc = gg_core.1.d
	flavor = gg_core.1.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_power_blocs"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = short_modifier_time }

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:GBR ?= this
			}
			c:RUS ?= {
				save_scope_as = opponent_scope
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:RUS ?= this
			}
			c:GBR ?= {
				save_scope_as = opponent_scope
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gg_core.1.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						c:GBR ?= THIS
						has_global_variable = great_game_gbr_progress
						global_var:great_game_gbr_progress <= 40
					}
					AND = {
						c:RUS ?= THIS
						has_global_variable = great_game_gbr_progress
						global_var:great_game_gbr_progress >= 160
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_great_game_victor
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						c:GBR ?= THIS
						has_global_variable = great_game_gbr_progress
						global_var:great_game_gbr_progress <= 80
					}
					AND = {
						c:RUS ?= THIS
						has_global_variable = great_game_gbr_progress
						global_var:great_game_gbr_progress >= 120
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_great_game_victor
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		else = { # Should never happen
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_great_game_victor
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
				multiplier = 0.5
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_player = yes
			}
			clear_great_game_variables = yes
		}
		scope:opponent_scope ?= {
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_positive
				target = root
			}
		}
	}
}

gg_core.2 = { # Great Game defeat
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = gg_core.2.t
	desc = gg_core.2.d
	flavor = gg_core.2.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_transfer_of_authority"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = short_modifier_time }

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:GBR ?= this
			}
			c:RUS ?= {
				save_scope_as = opponent_scope
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:RUS ?= this
			}
			c:GBR ?= {
				save_scope_as = opponent_scope
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gg_core.2.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						c:GBR ?= THIS
						has_global_variable = great_game_gbr_progress
						global_var:great_game_gbr_progress >= 160
					}
					AND = {
						c:RUS ?= THIS
						has_global_variable = great_game_gbr_progress
						global_var:great_game_gbr_progress <= 40
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_great_game_defeated
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
				multiplier = 1.5
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						c:GBR ?= THIS
						has_global_variable = great_game_gbr_progress
						global_var:great_game_gbr_progress >= 120
					}
					AND = {
						c:RUS ?= THIS
						has_global_variable = great_game_gbr_progress
						global_var:great_game_gbr_progress <= 80
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_great_game_defeated
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		else = { # Should never happen
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_great_game_defeated
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
				multiplier = 0.5
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_player = yes
			}
			clear_great_game_variables = yes
		}
		scope:opponent_scope ?= {
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_negative
				target = root
			}
		}
	}
}

﻿namespace = kazakhstan_events

kazakhstan_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/europenorthamerica_springtime_of_nations.bk2"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	title = kazakhstan_events.1.t
	desc = kazakhstan_events.1.d
	flavor = kazakhstan_events.1.f

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	trigger = {

	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:UZH
				c:UZH ?= {
					is_subject_of = c:RUS
				}
			}
			c:UZH ?= {
				change_tag = KAZ
				make_independent = yes
				end_truce = c:RUS
				random_scope_state = {
					save_scope_as = kenesary_state
				}
				save_scope_as = kenesary_country
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = s:STATE_SYRDARYA
					this.state_region = {
						is_homeland = cu:kazak
					}
					owner = {
						OR = {
							c:RUS ?= THIS
							is_subject_of = c:RUS
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = kenesary_state
				owner = {
					save_scope_as = kenesary_country
				}
			}
			create_country = {
				tag = KAZ
				origin = scope:kenesary_country
				state = scope:kenesary_state
				on_created = {

				}
			}
		}

		c:KAZ ?= {
			save_scope_as = kazakh_scope
			create_character = {
				template = kaz_kenesary_khan
			}
			ruler = {
				save_scope_as = kenesary_scope
			}
			activate_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
			activate_law = law_type:law_peasant_levies
			set_ruling_interest_groups = { ig_landowners ig_devout }
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_kenesary_revolt
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
			create_military_formation = {
				type = army
				hq_region = scope:kazakh_scope.capital.region

				combat_unit = {
					type = unit_type:combat_unit_type_irregular_infantry
					state_region = scope:kazakh_scope.capital.state_region
					count = 5
				}

				combat_unit = {
					type = unit_type:combat_unit_type_hussars
					state_region = scope:kazakh_scope.capital.state_region
					count = 5
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_AKMOLINSK = {
			add_claim = c:KAZ
		}
		s:STATE_URALSK = {
			add_claim = c:KAZ
		}
		s:STATE_KHIVA = {
			add_claim = c:KAZ
		}
		s:STATE_AKTOBE = {
			add_claim = c:KAZ
		}
		s:STATE_SYRDARYA = {
			add_claim = c:KAZ
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = kazakhstan_events.1.a
		add_journal_entry = { type = je_the_last_kazakh_khan }
        default_option = yes
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_SEMIRECHE
				owner = {
					OR = {
						is_subject_of = c:RUS
						c:RUS ?= THIS
					}
				}
			}
			set_state_owner = c:KAZ
			set_state_type = incorporated
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_SYRDARYA
				owner = {
					OR = {
						is_subject_of = c:RUS
						c:RUS ?= THIS
					}
				}
			}
			set_state_owner = c:KAZ
			set_state_type = incorporated
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:OZH ?= {
					liberty_desire > 60
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:OZH ?= {
						is_player = yes
					}
				}
				c:OZH ?= {
					play_as = c:KAZ
				}
			}
			c:KAZ ?= {
				annex = c:OZH
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:KZH ?= {
					liberty_desire > 60
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:KZH ?= {
						is_player = yes
					}
				}
				c:KZH ?= {
					play_as = c:KAZ
				}
			}
			c:KAZ ?= {
				annex = c:KZH
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:UZH ?= {
					liberty_desire > 60
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:UZH ?= {
						is_player = yes
					}
				}
				c:UZH ?= {
					play_as = c:KAZ
				}
			}
			c:KAZ ?= {
				annex = c:UZH
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:OZH
				exists = c:KZH
			}
			c:KAZ ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = annex_war
					target_country = c:OZH
					type = dp_annex_war

					add_target_backers = { c:KZH }
					add_war_goal = { holder = c:KAZ target_country = c:KZH type = annex_country }
					add_war_goal = { holder = c:RUS target_country = c:KAZ type = make_protectorate }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:OZH
				NOT = {
					exists = c:KZH
				}
			}
			c:KAZ ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = annex_war
					target_country = c:OZH
					type = dp_annex_war

					add_war_goal = { holder = c:KAZ target_state = s:STATE_URALSK.region_state:RUS type = return_state }
					add_war_goal = { holder = c:RUS target_country = c:KAZ type = make_protectorate }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:KZH
				NOT = {
					exists = c:OZH
				}
			}
			c:KAZ ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = annex_war
					target_country = c:KZH
					type = dp_annex_war

					add_war_goal = { holder = c:KAZ target_country = c:KZH type = annex_country }
					add_war_goal = { holder = c:KAZ target_state = s:STATE_AKMOLINSK.region_state:RUS type = return_state }
					add_war_goal = { holder = c:RUS target_country = c:KAZ type = make_protectorate }
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			c:KAZ ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = annex_war
					target_country = c:RUS
					type = dp_return_state
					target_state = s:STATE_AKMOLINSK.region_state:RUS

					add_war_goal = { holder = c:KAZ target_state = s:STATE_URALSK.region_state:RUS type = return_state }
					add_war_goal = { holder = c:RUS target_country = c:KAZ type = make_protectorate }
				}
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					OR = {
						c:RUS ?= THIS
						this = {
							is_subject_of = c:RUS
						}
					}
				}
				this.state_region = {
					is_homeland = cu:kazak
				}
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = kenesary_recognizer_var
			}
			change_relations = {
				country = c:KAZ
				value = 50
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					initiator = {
						c:KAZ ?= THIS
					}
				}
				add_initiator_backers = { prev }
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_subject_of = c:RUS
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:kazak
			}
			add_radicals = {
				value = large_radicals
				culture = cu:kazak
			}
			add_modifier = { name = modifier_kazakh_ungovernable_provinces days = long_modifier_time is_decaying = yes }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					owner = c:KAZ
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_taiping_hiring_bonus # Temporary bonus to fill their barracks
					months = 6
				}
			}
		}
	}

    option = {
		name = kazakhstan_events.1.b
		add_journal_entry = { type = je_the_last_kazakh_khan }
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_SEMIRECHE
				owner = {
					OR = {
						is_subject_of = c:RUS
						c:RUS ?= THIS
					}
				}
			}
			set_state_owner = c:KAZ
			set_state_type = incorporated
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_SYRDARYA
				owner = {
					OR = {
						is_subject_of = c:RUS
						c:RUS ?= THIS
					}
				}
			}
			set_state_owner = c:KAZ
			set_state_type = incorporated
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:OZH ?= {
					liberty_desire > 60
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:OZH ?= {
						is_player = yes
					}
				}
				c:OZH ?= {
					play_as = c:KAZ
				}
			}
			c:KAZ ?= {
				annex = c:OZH
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:KZH ?= {
					liberty_desire > 60
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:KZH ?= {
						is_player = yes
					}
				}
				c:KZH ?= {
					play_as = c:KAZ
				}
			}
			c:KAZ ?= {
				annex = c:KZH
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:UZH ?= {
					liberty_desire > 60
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:UZH ?= {
						is_player = yes
					}
				}
				c:UZH ?= {
					play_as = c:KAZ
				}
			}
			c:KAZ ?= {
				annex = c:UZH
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:OZH
				exists = c:KZH
			}
			c:KAZ ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = annex_war
					target_country = c:OZH
					type = dp_annex_war

					add_target_backers = { c:KZH }
					add_war_goal = { holder = c:KAZ target_country = c:KZH type = annex_country }
					add_war_goal = { holder = c:RUS target_country = c:KAZ type = make_protectorate }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:OZH
				NOT = {
					exists = c:KZH
				}
			}
			c:KAZ ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = annex_war
					target_country = c:OZH
					type = dp_annex_war

					add_war_goal = { holder = c:KAZ target_state = s:STATE_URALSK.region_state:RUS type = return_state }
					add_war_goal = { holder = c:RUS target_country = c:KAZ type = make_protectorate }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:KZH
				NOT = {
					exists = c:OZH
				}
			}
			c:KAZ ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = annex_war
					target_country = c:KZH
					type = dp_annex_war

					add_war_goal = { holder = c:KAZ target_country = c:KZH type = annex_country }
					add_war_goal = { holder = c:KAZ target_state = s:STATE_AKMOLINSK.region_state:RUS type = return_state }
					add_war_goal = { holder = c:RUS target_country = c:KAZ type = make_protectorate }
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			c:KAZ ?= {
				create_diplomatic_play = {
					name = annex_war
					target_country = c:RUS
					type = dp_return_state
					target_state = s:STATE_AKMOLINSK.region_state:RUS

					add_war_goal = { holder = c:KAZ target_state = s:STATE_URALSK.region_state:RUS type = return_state }
					add_war_goal = { holder = c:RUS target_country = c:KAZ type = make_protectorate }
				}
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = kenesary_recognizer_var
			}
			change_relations = {
				country = c:KAZ
				value = 50
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					initiator = {
						c:KAZ ?= THIS
					}
				}
				add_initiator_backers = { prev }
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_subject_of = c:RUS
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:kazak
			}
			add_radicals = {
				value = large_radicals
				culture = cu:kazak
			}
			add_modifier = { name = modifier_kazakh_ungovernable_provinces days = long_modifier_time is_decaying = yes }
		}
		play_as = c:KAZ
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					owner = c:KAZ
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = modifier_taiping_hiring_bonus # Temporary bonus to fill their barracks
					months = 6
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

russian_central_asia.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/ep1_ca_mountain_pass_military.bk2"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	title = russian_central_asia.1.t
	desc = russian_central_asia.1.d
	flavor = russian_central_asia.1.f

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		#Triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { name = count_casian_countries value = 0 }
        every_country = {
			limit = {
				capital = {
					region = sr:region_central_asia
				}
			}
			ROOT = {
				change_variable = { name = count_casian_countries add = 1 }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = russian_central_asia.1.a
        default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = central_asia_influence_tt
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_central_asian_influence
			days = normal_modifier_time
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_medium
		}
        every_country = {
			limit = {
				capital = {
					region = sr:region_central_asia
				}
			}
			change_relations = {
				country = c:RUS
				value = 10
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_central_asian_bribe_payments
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_casian_bribes
			}
			post_notification = central_asia_bribes_notification
		}
		remove_variable = count_casian_countries
	}

	option = {
		name = russian_central_asia.1.b
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				capital = {
					region = sr:region_central_asia
				}
				NOT = {
					is_subject_of = c:RUS
				}
			}
			change_relations = {
				country = c:RUS
				value = -20
			}
		}
		remove_variable = count_casian_countries
	}
}

russian_central_asia.2 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/ep1_octopus.bk2"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	title = russian_central_asia.2.t
	desc = russian_central_asia.2.d
	flavor = russian_central_asia.2.f

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		#Triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		ordered_country = {
        	limit = {
        		is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_europe
        		OR = {
        			any_scope_state = {
        				OR = {
							region = sr:region_punjab
							region = sr:region_bombay
							region = sr:region_madras
							region = sr:region_central_india
							region = sr:region_bengal
    					}
						count >= 3
        			}
        			any_country = {
        				is_subject_of = prev
        				any_scope_state = {
        					OR = {
 								region = sr:region_punjab
								region = sr:region_bombay
								region = sr:region_madras
								region = sr:region_central_india
								region = sr:region_bengal
        					}
							count >= 3
	        			}
        			}
        		}
        	}
        	order_by = country_rank
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
        	save_scope_as = india_overlord
		}
		ordered_state = {
        	limit = {
        		state_region = s:STATE_KABUL
        	}
			order_by = owner.country_rank
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
        	save_scope_as = kabul_state
        	owner = {
        		save_scope_as = afghan_scope
        	}
        }
        if = {
        	limit = {
        		any_interest_group = {
		        	NOR = {
	        			leader = {
	        				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_pacifist
	        			}
	        			has_ideology = ideology:ideology_isolationist
	        		}
	        		law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_no_colonial_affairs
						value <= neutral
					}
		        }
        	}
        	random_interest_group = {
        		limit = {
        			NOR = {
	        			leader = {
	        				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_pacifist
	        			}
	        			has_ideology = ideology:ideology_isolationist
	        		}
	        		law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_no_colonial_affairs
						value <= neutral
					}
        		}
        		save_scope_as = hawkish_ig
        	}
        }
        else = {
        	ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
        		save_scope_as = hawkish_ig
        	}
        }
	}

	option = {
		name = russian_central_asia.2.a
        default_option = yes
        trigger = {
        	exists = c:AFG
        }
        every_country_in_europe = {
        	limit = {
        		OR = {
        			any_scope_state = {
        				OR = {
							region = sr:region_punjab
							region = sr:region_bombay
							region = sr:region_madras
							region = sr:region_central_india
							region = sr:region_bengal
    					}
						count >= 3
        			}
        			any_country = {
        				is_subject_of = prev
        				any_scope_state = {
        					OR = {
  								region = sr:region_punjab
								region = sr:region_bombay
								region = sr:region_madras
								region = sr:region_central_india
								region = sr:region_bengal
        					}
							count >= 3
	        			}
        			}
        		}
        	}
        	change_relations = {
        		country = c:RUS
        		value = -20
        	}
		}
		create_incident = {
			country = c:AFG
			value = 20
		}
		every_state_region = {
			limit = {
				is_homeland = cu:pashtun
			}
			add_claim = c:RUS
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = russian_central_asia.2.b
        default_option = yes
        add_modifier = {
        	name = modifier_suzerain_of_central_asia
        	days = long_modifier_time
        }
    	change_relations = {
    		country = scope:afghan_scope
    		value = 20
    	}
	}
}

russian_central_asia.3 = {
	type = country_event

	title = russian_central_asia.3.t
	desc = russian_central_asia.3.d
	flavor = russian_central_asia.3.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_ca_mountain_pass_military"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		# triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		c:TRM ?= {
			save_scope_as = turkmenia_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = russian_central_asia.3.a
        default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_law = law_type:law_frontier_colonization
				}
			}
			activate_law = law_type:law_frontier_colonization
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_turkmen_subjugation
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_turkmen_subjugation
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_turkmen_logistics
			days = normal_modifier_time
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_very_large
		}
		add_radicals = {
			culture = cu:turkmen
			value = medium_radicals
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = { region = sr:region_central_asia }
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				culture = cu:russian
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}
}

gg_korea.2 = { # Donghak Rebellion
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = gg_korea.2.t
	desc = gg_korea.2.d
	flavor = gg_korea.2.f

	dlc = dlc010

		video = "ep1_donghak_rebellion"
	}

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	duration = 3

	immediate = {
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			save_scope_as = donghak_scope
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				is_insurrectionary = yes
				any_influenced_interest_group = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_rural_folk
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = revolution_movement
		}
		random_civil_war = {
			save_scope_as = ongoing_revolution
		}
		ordered_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_in_government = yes
			}
			order_by = ig_clout
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = gov_leader_scope
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gg_korea.2.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:revolution_movement = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_donghak_peasant_revolts
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:ongoing_revolution = {
			add_civil_war_progress = 0.50
		}
	}
}

gg_korea.3 = { # Choe Je-u
	type = country_event

	placement = scope:choi_je_woo_state

	flavor = gg_korea.3.f

		has_dlc_feature = ep1_content
		game_date > 1855.1.1
		game_date < 1880.1.1
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:korean

			has_global_variable = choi_je_woo_spawn
		}
		has_technology_researched = egalitarianism

			radical_fraction = {
				value >= 0.1
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		create_character = {
			template = KOR_choi_je_woo
			on_created = {
				set_variable = choi_je_woo_var
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = choi_je_woo_var
			}
			save_scope_as = choi_je_woo_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				radical_fraction = {
					value >= 0.1
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = choi_je_woo_state
		}
		set_global_variable = choi_je_woo_spawn
	}

	option = {
		name = gg_korea.3.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:choi_je_woo_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_donghak_founder
				days = very_long_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:choi_je_woo_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = small_radicals
				pop_type = peasants
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gg_korea.3.b
		scope:choi_je_woo_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_donghak_founder_hunted
				days = very_long_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:choi_je_woo_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = peasants
			}
		}
	}

gg_korea.4 = { # Donghak Petition
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = gg_korea.4.ta
				trigger = {
					scope:petition_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_YANGHO
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = gg_korea.4.tb
				trigger = {
					scope:petition_state = {
						NOT = {
							state_region = s:STATE_YANGHO
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	desc = gg_korea.4.d
	flavor = gg_korea.4.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_donghak_rebellion"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = donghak_petition_event_var
		}
		radical_fraction = {
			value >= 0.2
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			is_in_government = no
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
			save_scope_as = donghak_ig
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = s:STATE_YANGHO
					radical_fraction = {
						value >= 0.1
					}
				}
			}
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = s:STATE_YANGHO
					radical_fraction = {
						value >= 0.1
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = petition_state
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					radical_fraction = {
						value >= 0.1
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = petition_state
			}
		}
		set_variable = donghak_petition_event_var
	}

	option = {
		name = gg_korea.4.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:donghak_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_donghak_petition_rejected
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		scope:petition_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
				pop_type = peasants
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gg_korea.4.b
		add_journal_entry = { type = je_gyojo_shinwon }
	}
}

gg_korea.7 = { # Korean Uprising
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = gg_korea.7.ta
				trigger = {
					scope:korea_rebellion_scope = {
						ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
							is_in_government = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = gg_korea.7.tb
				trigger = {
					scope:korea_rebellion_scope = {
						NOT = {
							ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
								is_in_government = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = gg_korea.7.d.japan
				trigger = {
					c:JAP ?= this
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = gg_korea.7.d.china
				trigger = {
					c:KOR ?= {
						is_subject_of = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	flavor = gg_korea.7.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_opium_smoker"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	duration = 3

	immediate = {
		c:KOR ?= {
			save_scope_as = korea_scope
			overlord = {
				save_scope_as = china_scope
			}
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				civil_war_origin_country = scope:korea_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = korea_rebellion_scope
			capital = {
				save_scope_as = korea_rebellion_capital
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Side with the Korean government
		name = gg_korea.7.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = yes
			}
			set_secret_goal = {
				country = scope:korea_rebellion_scope
				secret_goal = conquer
			}
			set_secret_goal = {
				country = scope:korea_scope
				secret_goal = protect
			}
		}
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_korean_rebellion
		}
		set_variable = sided_with_korean_gov
		scope:korea_scope ?= {
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_positive
				target = root
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Side with the rebels
		name = gg_korea.7.b
		trigger = {
			scope:korea_scope = {
				NOT = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
			}
			scope:korea_rebellion_scope = {
				NOT = {
					ig:ig_rural_folk ?= {
						is_in_government = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = yes
			}
			set_secret_goal = {
				country = scope:korea_scope
				secret_goal = conquer
			}
			set_secret_goal = {
				country = scope:korea_rebellion_scope
				secret_goal = protect
			}
		}
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_korean_rebellion
		}
		set_variable = sided_with_korean_rebels
	}

	option = { # Declare lack of interest
		name = gg_korea.7.c
		trigger = {
			scope:korea_scope = {
				NOT = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
	}
}

gg_korea.9 = { # Sino-Japanese War - for China
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = gg_korea.9.t
	desc = gg_korea.9.d
	flavor = gg_korea.9.f

	dlc = dlc010

	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_political_extremism"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {

	}

	immediate = {
		c:JAP ?= {
			save_scope_as = japan_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gg_korea.9.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:korea_scope = {
					has_diplomatic_pact = {
						who = scope:china_scope
						type = tributary
					}
				}
			}
			scope:korea_scope = {
				remove_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = scope:china_scope
					type = tributary
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:korea_scope = {
					has_diplomatic_pact = {
						who = scope:china_scope
						type = vassal
					}
				}
			}
			scope:korea_scope = {
				remove_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = scope:china_scope
					type = vassal
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:korea_scope = {
					has_diplomatic_pact = {
						who = scope:china_scope
						type = protectorate
					}
				}
			}
			scope:korea_scope = {
				remove_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = scope:china_scope
					type = protectorate
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:korea_scope = {
					has_diplomatic_pact = {
						who = scope:china_scope
						type = puppet
					}
				}
			}
			scope:korea_scope = {
				remove_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = scope:china_scope
					type = puppet
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:korea_scope = {
					has_diplomatic_pact = {
						who = scope:china_scope
						type = dominion
					}
				}
			}
			scope:korea_scope = {
				remove_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = scope:china_scope
					type = dominion
				}
			}
		}
		scope:korea_scope ?= {
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_negative
				target = root
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gg_korea.9.b
		change_relations = {
			country = c:JAP
			value = -50
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			set_secret_goal = {
				country = ROOT
				secret_goal = conquer
			}
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_negative
				target = root
			}
		}

korea_monarchy.1 = { # Hwan Yi dies
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = korea_monarchy.1.t
	desc = korea_monarchy.1.d
	flavor = korea_monarchy.1.f

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid

	left_icon = scope:heonjong_scope

	dlc = dlc010

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = heonjong_event_var
		}
		has_dlc_feature = ep1_content
		c:KOR ?= this
		game_date >= 1845.1.1
		ruler = {
			has_template = KOR_yi_hwan
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ruler = {
			save_scope_as = heonjong_scope
		}
		set_variable = heonjong_event_var
	}

	option = {
		name = korea_monarchy.1.a
		default_option = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
		scope:heonjong_scope = {
			kill_character = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = korea_monarchy.1.b
		trigger = {
			has_technology_researched = medical_degrees
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
		scope:heonjong_scope = {
			random_list = {
				75 = {
					kill_character = yes
				}
				25 = {
					custom_tooltip = {
						text = heonjong_recovers_tt
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

korea_monarchy.3 = { # Queen Myeongseong
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT

	title = korea_monarchy.3.t
	desc = korea_monarchy.3.d
	flavor = korea_monarchy.3.f

	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid

	left_icon = scope:myeongseong_scope

	dlc = dlc010

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = myeongseong_event_var
		}
		has_dlc_feature = ep1_content
		c:KOR ?= this
		game_date >= 1873.1.1
		ruler = {
			has_template = KOR_yi_myongbok
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		ruler = {
			save_scope_as = gojong_scope
		}
		create_character = {
			template = KOR_empress_myeongseong
			on_created = {
				set_variable = myeongseong_var
				place_character_in_void = 6
			}
		}
		random_character_in_void = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = myeongseong_var
			}
			save_scope_as = myeongseong_scope
		}
		set_variable = myeongseong_event_var
	}

	option = {
		name = korea_monarchy.3.a
		default_option = yes
		show_as_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				template = KOR_empress_myeongseong
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_character_in_void = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = myeongseong_var
				}
				free_character_from_void = yes
				set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
					is_in_government = no
				}
			}
			ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
				add_ruling_interest_group = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = korea_monarchy.3.b
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_empress_myeongseong
			days = very_long_modifier_time
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_character_in_void = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = myeongseong_var
				}
				kill_character = {
					hidden = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

persia_events.1 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:herat_scope

	title = persia_events.1.t
	desc = persia_events.1.d
	flavor = persia_events.1.f

		video = "ep1_persian_court"
	}

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"

		# triggered by JE
	}

			}

        default_option = yes

		name = persia_events.1.b

        	name = modifier_persianize_the_east
        	days = very_long_modifier_time

        	limit = {
        		any_scope_state = {
        			state_region = s:STATE_IRAKAJEMI
        		}
        	}
        	random_scope_state = {
	        	limit = {
	        		state_region = s:STATE_IRAKAJEMI
	        	}

		        	name = darulfunun_modifier
		        }
        	}
        }
        else = {

		        	name = darulfunun_modifier
		        }
        	}
        }
	}

	option = {
		name = persia_events.1.c

        	name = modifier_military_of_the_east
        	days = very_long_modifier_time
        }

        	add_claim = root

        	add_claim = root

        	add_claim = root

        	add_claim = root
        }
        s:STATE_ERZURUM = {
        	add_claim = root

        	add_claim = root

        s:STATE_GREATER_CAUCASUS = {

persia_events.2 = {
	type = country_event

		# triggered by JE
	}

				}
			}

				limit = {
					state_region = s:STATE_HERAT

			}
		}
		else = {
			limit = {
				any_subject_or_below = {
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_HERAT
					}
				}
			}

				limit = {
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_HERAT
					}
				}

					limit = {
						state_region = s:STATE_HERAT
					}
					save_scope_as = herat_scope

		}

	option = {

				add_claim = root
			}

		if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_BALKH = {
					any_scope_state = {
						NOT = {
							has_claim_by = root
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					country_or_subject_owns_entire_state_region = STATE_BALKH
				}
			}
			s:STATE_BALKH = {
				add_claim = root
			}
		}
		if = {

						}

		if = {

	}

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		# triggered by JE
	}

	immediate = {
		c:TRM ?= {
			save_scope_as = turkmenia_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = persia_events.3.a
        default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_law = law_type:law_frontier_colonization
				}
			}
			activate_law = law_type:law_frontier_colonization
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_turkmen_subjugation
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_turkmen_subjugation
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_turkmen_logistics
			days = normal_modifier_time
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_very_large
		}
		add_radicals = {
			culture = cu:turkmen
			value = medium_radicals
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			culture = cu:persian
			value = small_radicals
		}
	}
}

			}

			limit = {
				region = sr:region_central_asia

					culture = cu:persian
					is_pop_type = slaves
				}

					has_variable = persian_slave_liberate_var
				}

		}

			save_scope_as = persian_culture
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = persia_events.5.a
        default_option = yes
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_central_asia
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:persian
					is_pop_type = slaves
				}
				NOT = {
					has_variable = persian_slave_liberate_var
				}
			}
			every_scope_pop = {
				limit = {
					culture = cu:persian
					is_pop_type = slaves
				}
				change_poptype = pop_type:farmers
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_pop = {
						culture = cu:turkmen
					}
				}
				add_radicals_in_state = {
					culture = cu:turkmen
					value = medium_radicals
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_pop = {
						culture = cu:turkmen
					}
				}
				add_radicals_in_state = {
					culture = cu:uzbek
					value = medium_radicals
				}
			}
			set_variable = persian_slave_liberate_var
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = persia_events.5.b
		custom_tooltip = persia_events.5.tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
					has_decree = decree_emergency_relief
				}
			}
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					has_decree = decree_emergency_relief
				}
				save_scope_as = persian_migration_target_scope
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
					OR = {
						state_region = s:STATE_KHORASAN
						state_region = s:STATE_MAZANDARAN
						state_region = s:STATE_HERAT
					}
				}
			}
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						state_region = s:STATE_KHORASAN
						state_region = s:STATE_MAZANDARAN
						state_region = s:STATE_HERAT
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = persian_migration_target_scope
			}
		}
		else = {
			capital = {
				save_scope_as = persian_migration_target_scope
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_central_asia
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:persian
					is_pop_type = slaves
				}
				NOT = {
					has_variable = persian_slave_liberate_var
				}
			}
			show_as_tooltip = {
				every_scope_pop = {
					limit = {
						culture = cu:persian
						is_pop_type = slaves
					}
					change_poptype = pop_type:farmers
					move_pop = scope:persian_migration_target_scope
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				every_scope_pop = {
					limit = {
						culture = cu:persian
						is_pop_type = slaves
					}
					change_poptype = pop_type:farmers
				}
				every_scope_pop = {
					limit = {
						culture = cu:persian
						is_pop_type = farmers
					}
					move_pop = scope:persian_migration_target_scope
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_pop = {
						culture = cu:turkmen
					}
				}
				add_radicals_in_state = {
					culture = cu:turkmen
					value = medium_radicals
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_pop = {
						culture = cu:turkmen
					}
				}
				add_radicals_in_state = {
					culture = cu:uzbek
					value = medium_radicals
				}
			}
			set_variable = persian_slave_liberate_var
		}
		scope:persian_migration_target_scope = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_slave_relocation_costs_persia
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_medium
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = persia_events.5.c
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_central_asia
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:persian
					is_pop_type = slaves
				}
				NOT = {
					has_variable = persian_slave_liberate_var
				}
			}
			set_variable = persian_slave_liberate_var
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_pop = {
						culture = cu:turkmen
					}
				}
				add_loyalists_in_state = {
					culture = cu:turkmen
					value = medium_radicals
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_pop = {
						culture = cu:turkmen
					}
				}
				add_loyalists_in_state = {
					culture = cu:uzbek
					value = medium_radicals
				}
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				culture = cu:persian
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}
}

persia_events.6 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:herat_scope

	title = persia_events.6.t
	desc = persia_events.6.d
	flavor = persia_events.6.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_ca_mountain_pass_military"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		# triggered by event
	}

	immediate = {
		c:PER ?= {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = s:STATE_HERAT
				}
				save_scope_as = herat_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = persia_scope
		}
		save_scope_as = britain_scope
	}

	option = {
		name = persia_events.6.a
        default_option = yes
		c:PER ?= {
			trigger_event = { id = persia_events.8 popup = yes }
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_negative
				target = root
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:PER
			value = -50
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = persia_events.6.b
		c:PER ?= {
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_positive
				target = root
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:PER
			value = 20
		}
	}
}
