persia_events.8 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:herat_scope

	title = persia_events.8.t
	desc = persia_events.8.d
	flavor = persia_events.8.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_redcoats"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_military.dds"

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	trigger = {
		# triggered by event
	}

	immediate = {
		c:GBR ?= {
			save_scope_as = britain_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_HERAT
			}
			save_scope_as = herat_scope
		}
		if = {
        	limit = {
	        	c:HER ?= {
	        		exists = no
	        	}
	        	c:GBR ?= {
	        		any_subject_or_below = {
						OR = {
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:tajik
						}
	        		}
	        	}
	        }
	        c:GBR ?= {
		        random_subject_or_below = {
		        	limit = {
		        		OR = {
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:tajik
						}
		        	}
		        	save_scope_as = british_afghan_subject
		        }
		    }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = persia_events.8.a
        default_option = yes
        custom_tooltip = {
        	text = britain_wants_herat_tt
			scope:britain_scope = {
				set_secret_goal = {
					country = root
					secret_goal = conquer
				}
			}
			set_secret_goal = {
				country = scope:britain_scope
				secret_goal = antagonize
			}
        }
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_landowners
					is_interest_group_type = ig_devout
					is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = did_not_bow_to_britain_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 7
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = persia_events.8.b
        if = {
        	limit = {
        		c:HER ?= {
        			NOT = {
        				is_subject_of = root
        			}
        		}
        	}
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = s:STATE_HERAT
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_modifier = modifier_herat_inspiration
					}
					remove_modifier = modifier_herat_inspiration
				}
				set_state_owner = c:HER
			}
			c:HER ?= {
        		change_relations = {
					country = c:GBR
					value = 50
				}
			}
        }
        else_if = {
        	limit = {
	        	NOT = {
					exists = c:HER
				}
	        }
        	create_country = {
        		tag = HER
        		origin = root
        		state = scope:herat_scope
        		on_created = {
        			change_relations = {
						country = c:GBR
						value = 50
					}
        		}
        	}
        }
        else_if = {
        	limit = {
	        	NOT = {
					exists = c:HER
				}
	        	c:GBR ?= {
	        		any_subject_or_below = {
						OR = {
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:pashtun
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:tajik
						}
	        		}
	        	}
	        }
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = s:STATE_HERAT
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_modifier = modifier_herat_inspiration
					}
					remove_modifier = modifier_herat_inspiration
				}
				set_state_owner = scope:british_afghan_subject
			}
        }
        else = {
			create_dynamic_country = {
				origin = root
				country_type = unrecognized
				culture = cu:persian
				religion = rel:sunni
				tier = principality
				capital = scope:herat_scope
				cede_state_trigger = {
					state_region = s:STATE_HERAT
				}
				on_created = {
        			change_relations = {
						country = c:GBR
						value = 50
					}
				}
			}
        }
		change_relations = {
			country = c:GBR
			value = 10
		}
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_interest_group_type = ig_landowners
					is_interest_group_type = ig_devout
					is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = bowed_to_britain_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 3
		}
	}
}
﻿namespace = lobby_events

lobby_events.21 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_clandestine_meeting"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	title = lobby_events.21.t
	desc = lobby_events.21.d
	flavor = lobby_events.21.f

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		any_interest_group = {
			is_member_of_any_lobby = no
			leader = {
				OR = { # is patriotically inclined
					has_reactionary_ideology = yes
					has_conservative_ideology = yes
					has_monarchist_ideology = yes
				}
			}
		}
		any_political_lobby = {
			is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
			target = {
				any_diplomatically_relevant_country = {
					NOT = {
						this = root
					}
					OR = {
						has_diplomatic_pact = {
							who = root
							type = rivalry
						}
						any_scope_state = {
							has_claim_by = root
						}
					}
					OR = {
						has_diplomatic_pact = {
							who = prev
							type = rivalry
						}
						any_scope_state = {
							has_claim_by = prev
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_member_of_any_lobby = no
				leader = {
					OR = { # is patriotically inclined
						has_reactionary_ideology = yes
						has_conservative_ideology = yes
						has_monarchist_ideology = yes
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_ig_scope
		}
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
				target = {
					any_diplomatically_relevant_country = {
						NOT = {
							this = root
						}
						OR = {
							has_diplomatic_pact = {
								who = root
								type = rivalry
							}
							any_scope_state = {
								has_claim_by = root
							}
						}
						OR = {
							has_diplomatic_pact = {
								who = prev
								type = rivalry
							}
							any_scope_state = {
								has_claim_by = prev
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_scope
			target = {
				random_diplomatically_relevant_country = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							this = root
						}
						OR = {
							has_diplomatic_pact = {
								who = root
								type = rivalry
							}
							any_scope_state = {
								has_claim_by = root
							}
						}
						OR = {
							has_diplomatic_pact = {
								who = prev
								type = rivalry
							}
							any_scope_state = {
								has_claim_by = prev
							}
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = lobby_target_scope
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_country_scope
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_events.21.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby_scope = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig_scope
		}
		scope:relevant_lobby_scope = {
			change_appeasement = {
			   amount = 3
			   factor = appeasement_lobby_pleased
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:lobby_target_scope value = -20
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = lobby_events.21.b
		scope:relevant_lobby_scope = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = lobby_events.21.c
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				scope:relevant_lobby_scope = {
					add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig_scope
				}
			}
			9 = {
				scope:relevant_ig_scope = {
					add_modifier = {
						name = enemy_of_enemy_ig_modifier
						days = normal_modifier_time
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:relevant_lobby_scope = {
			change_appeasement = {
			   amount = -3
			   factor = appeasement_lobby_displeased
			}
		}
	}
}

lobby_events.22 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_printing_press"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	title = lobby_events.22.t
	desc = lobby_events.22.d
	flavor = lobby_events.22.f

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		OR = {
			country_has_voting_franchise = no
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_landed_voting
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_census_voting
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_census_voting
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_wealth_voting
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_wealth_voting
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
					}
				}
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
					}
				}
				target = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_country_scope
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_scope
			}
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_ig_scope
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_census_voting
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_census_voting
					}
				}
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_census_voting
					}
				}
				target = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_country_scope
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_scope
			}
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_census_voting
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_ig_scope
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_wealth_voting
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_wealth_voting
					}
				}
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_wealth_voting
					}
				}
				target = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_country_scope
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_scope
			}
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_wealth_voting
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_ig_scope
			}
		}
		scope:relevant_country_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
				}
				random_law = {
					limit = {
						type = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
					}
					save_scope_as = relevant_law_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_census_voting
				}
				random_law = {
					limit = {
						type = law_type:law_census_voting
					}
					save_scope_as = relevant_law_scope
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_wealth_voting
				}
				random_law = {
					limit = {
						type = law_type:law_wealth_voting
					}
					save_scope_as = relevant_law_scope
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_events.22.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby_scope = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = lobby_events.22.b
		add_radicals = {
			value = small_radicals
			interest_group = scope:relevant_ig_scope
		}
	}
}

lobby_events.23 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_persian_court"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	title = lobby_events.23.t
	desc = lobby_events.23.d
	flavor = lobby_events.23.f

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		OR = {
			AND = { # Autrocratic or Enlightened monarch
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_monarchy
						value > neutral
					}
					OR = {
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_autocracy
							value > neutral
						}
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_technocracy
							value > neutral
						}
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
						OR = {
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_autocracy
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_technocracy
						}
						ruler = {
							has_desirable_character_traits = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			AND = { # Presidential strongman
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_presidential_republic
						value > neutral
					}
					OR = {
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_autocracy
							value > neutral
						}
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_technocracy
							value > neutral
						}
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_presidential_republic
						OR = {
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_autocracy
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_technocracy
						}
						ruler = {
							has_desirable_character_traits = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_monarchy
						value > neutral
					}
					OR = {
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_autocracy
							value > neutral
						}
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_technocracy
							value > neutral
						}
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
						OR = {
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_autocracy
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_technocracy
						}
						ruler = {
							has_desirable_character_traits = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_monarchy
						value > neutral
					}
					OR = {
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_autocracy
							value > neutral
						}
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_technocracy
							value > neutral
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_ig_scope
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
						OR = {
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_autocracy
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_technocracy
						}
						ruler = {
							has_desirable_character_traits = yes
						}
					}
				}
				target = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_country_scope
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_scope
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_presidential_republic
						value > neutral
					}
					OR = {
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_autocracy
							value > neutral
						}
						law_stance = {
							law = law_type:law_technocracy
							value > neutral
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_ig_scope
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_presidential_republic
						OR = {
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_autocracy
							has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_technocracy
						}
						ruler = {
							has_desirable_character_traits = yes
						}
					}
				}
				target = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_country_scope
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_scope
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_events.23.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby_scope = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			scope:relevant_country_scope.ruler = {
				culture = {
					root = {
						cultural_acceptance_base = { target = prev value <= acceptance_status_4 }
					}
				}
			}
			scope:relevant_ig_scope = {
				leader = {
					NOR = {
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian
						has_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian_royalist
					}
				}
			}
		}
		highlighted_option = yes
		name = lobby_events.23.b
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:relevant_country_scope value = -20
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = racist_rhetorics_modifier
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
}

lobby_events.24 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_redcoats"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	title = lobby_events.24.t
	desc = lobby_events.24.d
	flavor = lobby_events.24.f

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = normal_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		any_interest_group = {
			is_member_of_any_lobby = no
			is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces
		}
		any_political_lobby = {
			is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
			target = {
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = root
				}
				techs_researched > {
				    value = root.techs_researched
				    multiply = 1.5
				}
				OR = {
					army_size > {
					    value = root.army_size
					    multiply = 1.5
					}
					navy_size > {
					    value = root.navy_size
					    multiply = 1.5
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_member_of_any_lobby = no
				is_interest_group_type = ig_armed_forces
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_ig_scope
		}
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
				target = {
					NOT = {
						has_war_with = root
					}
					techs_researched > {
					    value = root.techs_researched
					    multiply = 1.5
					}
					OR = {
						army_size > {
						    value = root.army_size
						    multiply = 1.5
						}
						navy_size > {
						    value = root.navy_size
						    multiply = 1.5
						}
					}
				}
			}
			target = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						army_size > {
						    value = root.army_size
						    multiply = 1.5
						}
					}
					set_variable = {
						name = army_is_strongest_var
						days = short_modifier_time
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_country_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_events.24.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby_scope = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = lobby_events.24.b
		add_modifier = {
			name = officer_corps_unrest_modifier
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		scope:relevant_ig_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = affiliation_ban_enforced_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

				is_member_of_any_lobby = no
				law_stance = {
					law = law_type:law_census_voting
					value > neutral
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_ig_scope
		}
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
				target = {
					country_has_voting_franchise = no
					NOT = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic
					}
				}
			}
			target = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_country_scope
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_scope
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_events.29.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby_scope = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = lobby_events.29.b
		scope:relevant_ig_scope = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = in_league_with_tyrants_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

					}
				}

				save_scope_as = relevant_country_scope

		}

			add_modifier = {
				name = lodged_diplomatic_complaint_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:relevant_country_scope value = -10
		}
	}
}

# Impressive Tax Reform
lobby_events.40 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.40.t
	desc = lobby_events.40.d
	flavor = lobby_events.40.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_signed_contract"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
					}
				}
			}
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_ig
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_graduated_taxation
					}
				}
				target = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			}
		}
		else = {
			limit = {
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
					}
				}
			}
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_ig
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_proportional_taxation
					}
				}
				target = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			}
		}
		currently_enacting_law = {
    		save_scope_as = relevant_law_scope
    	}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:relevant_law_scope }
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby_country }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_events.40.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig
			change_appeasement = {
			   amount = 1
			   factor = appeasement_lobby_pleased
			}
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = ig_happy_with_law_enactment
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = lobby_events.40.b
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig
		}
		scope:relevant_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = ig_join_pol_str_positive
				days = short_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		add_loyalists = {
			value = small_radicals
			interest_group = scope:relevant_ig
		}
	}
}

# Differences Aside
lobby_events.31 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.31.t
	desc = lobby_events.31.d
	flavor = lobby_events.31.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_clandestine_meeting"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		OR = {
			AND = { # IG with progressive or liberal IG leader joins lobby that has a conservative or reactionary member
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_progressive_ideology = yes
							has_liberal_ideology = yes
							has_socialist_ideology = yes
						}
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = { # existing member has opposing ideology
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = { has_technology_researched = film }
					any_lobby_member = {
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_conservative_ideology = yes
								has_reactionary_ideology = yes
								has_monarchist_ideology = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			AND = { # IG with conservative or reactionary IG leader joins lobby that has a progressive or liberal member
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_conservative_ideology = yes
							has_reactionary_ideology = yes
							has_monarchist_ideology = yes
						}
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = { # existing member has opposing ideology
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = { has_technology_researched = film }
					any_lobby_member = {
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_progressive_ideology = yes
								has_liberal_ideology = yes
								has_socialist_ideology = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_progressive_ideology = yes
							has_liberal_ideology = yes
							has_socialist_ideology = yes
						}
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = { # existing member has opposing ideology
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = { has_technology_researched = film }
					any_lobby_member = {
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_conservative_ideology = yes
								has_reactionary_ideology = yes
								has_monarchist_ideology = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_progressive_ideology = yes
							has_liberal_ideology = yes
							has_socialist_ideology = yes
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_ig
				leader = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_ig_leader
				}
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = { has_technology_researched = film }
					any_lobby_member = {
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_conservative_ideology = yes
								has_reactionary_ideology = yes
								has_monarchist_ideology = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				random_lobby_member = {
					limit ={
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_conservative_ideology = yes
								has_reactionary_ideology = yes
								has_monarchist_ideology = yes
							}
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_member
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
				target = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			limit = {
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_conservative_ideology = yes
							has_reactionary_ideology = yes
							has_monarchist_ideology = yes
						}
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = { # existing member has opposing ideology
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = { has_technology_researched = film }
					any_lobby_member = {
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_progressive_ideology = yes
								has_liberal_ideology = yes
								has_socialist_ideology = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_conservative_ideology = yes
							has_reactionary_ideology = yes
							has_monarchist_ideology = yes
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_ig
				leader = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_ig_leader
				}
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = { has_technology_researched = film }
					any_lobby_member = {
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_progressive_ideology = yes
								has_liberal_ideology = yes
								has_socialist_ideology = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				random_lobby_member = {
					limit ={
						leader = {
							OR = {
								has_progressive_ideology = yes
								has_liberal_ideology = yes
								has_socialist_ideology = yes
							}
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_member
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
				target = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby_country }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_events.31.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig
			change_appeasement = {
			   amount = 2
			   factor = appeasement_lobby_pleased
			}
		}
		scope:relevant_lobby_member = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = ig_join_pol_str_positive
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = lobby_events.31.b
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig
		}
		scope:relevant_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = ig_join_pol_str_negative
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}

# A Shining Example
lobby_events.32 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.32.t
	desc = lobby_events.32.d
	flavor = lobby_events.32.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_ruler_speaking_to_people"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_map.dds"

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		any_interest_group = {
			is_member_of_any_lobby = no
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			leader = {
				OR = {
					has_conservative_ideology = yes
					has_reactionary_ideology = yes
				}
			}
		}
		any_political_lobby = {
			is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
			target = {
				OR = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_charitable_health_system
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_religious_schools
				}
				ruler = {
					OR = {
						has_conservative_ideology = yes
						has_reactionary_ideology = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_member_of_any_lobby = no
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				leader = {
					OR = {
						has_conservative_ideology = yes
						has_reactionary_ideology = yes
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_ig
		}
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
				target = {
					OR = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_charitable_health_system
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_religious_schools
					}
					ruler = {
						OR = {
							has_conservative_ideology = yes
							has_reactionary_ideology = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			target = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby_country }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_events.32.a
		default_option = yes
			scope:relevant_lobby = {
				add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig
			}
			scope:relevant_ig = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = ig_join_pol_str_positive
					days = normal_modifier_time
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
	}
	option = {
		name = lobby_events.32.b
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			change_appeasement = {
			   amount = -2
			   factor = appeasement_lobby_displeased
			}
		}
		scope:relevant_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = lobby_events_ig_blocked
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			pop_type = clergymen
			religion = root.religion
			value = medium_radicals
		}
	}
}

# Less Is More
lobby_events.33 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.33.t
	desc = lobby_events.33.d
	flavor = lobby_events.33.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_politicians_arguing"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		any_political_lobby = {
			OR = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
			}
			any_lobby_member = {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				count >= 3
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
				}
				any_lobby_member = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					count >= 3
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			random_lobby_member = {
				limit = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_ig
			}
			random_lobby_member = { # another ig in the same lobby
				limit = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					NOT = { this = scope:relevant_ig }
				}
				save_scope_as = other_ig
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby_country }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_events.33.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			remove_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig
		}
		scope:other_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = ig_join_pol_str_positive
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # appears if relevant_ig is not in government
		name = lobby_events.33.b
		trigger = {
			scope:relevant_ig = {
				is_in_government = no
			}
		}
		scope:relevant_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = lobby_events_ig_blocked
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # appears if relevant_ig is in government
		name = lobby_events.33.c
		trigger = {
			scope:relevant_ig = {
				is_in_government = yes
			}
		}
		scope:relevant_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = lobby_events_ig_blocked
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = medium_radicals
			interest_group = scope:relevant_ig
		}
	}
}

# Laudable Legislation
lobby_events.34 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.34.t
	desc = lobby_events.34.d
	flavor = lobby_events.34.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_world_fair"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_slavery_banned
		any_political_lobby = {
			is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
			any_lobby_member = {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				leader = {
					OR = {
						has_progressive_ideology = yes
						has_liberal_ideology = yes
					}
				}
			}
			target = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_slavery_banned
					OR = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_protected_speech
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_womens_suffrage
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_guaranteed_liberties
					}
					ruler = {
						OR = {
							has_progressive_ideology = yes
							has_liberal_ideology = yes
						}
					}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
				target = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_slavery_banned
					OR = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_protected_speech
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_womens_suffrage
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_guaranteed_liberties
					}
					ruler = {
						OR = {
							has_progressive_ideology = yes
							has_liberal_ideology = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			target = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
			}
		}
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			random_lobby_member = {
				limit = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
					leader = {
						OR = {
							has_progressive_ideology = yes
							has_liberal_ideology = yes
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_ig
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby_country }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_events.34.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			remove_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = lobby_events.34.b
		add_radicals = {
			value = medium_radicals
			interest_group = scope:relevant_ig
		}
	}
}

# Unfavorable Opinion
lobby_events.35 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.35.t
	desc = lobby_events.35.d
	flavor = lobby_events.35.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_printing_press"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		any_interest_group = {
			is_member_of_any_lobby = no
			leader = {
				OR = {
					has_conservative_ideology = yes
					has_reactionary_ideology = yes
				}
			}
		}
		any_political_lobby = {
			is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
			target = { # law_slavery_banned AND one from the list
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_slavery_banned
				OR = { # one of five laws that are progressiveness = 100
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_womens_suffrage
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_cooperative_ownership
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_command_economy
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_protected_speech
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_guaranteed_liberties
				}
				ruler = {
					OR = {
						has_progressive_ideology = yes
						has_liberal_ideology = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_member_of_any_lobby = no
				leader = {
					OR = {
						has_conservative_ideology = yes
						has_reactionary_ideology = yes
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_ig
		}
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
				target = {
					# law_slavery_banned AND one from the list
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_slavery_banned
					OR = { # one of five laws that are progressiveness = 100
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_womens_suffrage
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_cooperative_ownership
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_command_economy
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_protected_speech
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_guaranteed_liberties
					}
					ruler = {
						OR = {
							has_progressive_ideology = yes
							has_liberal_ideology = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			target = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
				ruler.interest_group = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_ruler_ig
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby_country }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_events.35.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig
			change_appeasement = {
			   amount = 2
			   factor = appeasement_lobby_pleased
			}
		}
		scope:relevant_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = ig_join_pol_str_positive
				days = short_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = lobby_events.35.b
		add_radicals = {
			value = small_radicals
			interest_group = scope:relevant_ig
		}
	}
}

# Against Free Trade Proposal
lobby_events.36 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.36.t
	desc = lobby_events.36.d
	flavor = lobby_events.36.f

	event_image = {
		video = "southamerica_aristocrats"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = { # Check if free trade is being enacted
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		is_enacting_law = law_type:law_free_trade
		any_interest_group = {
			is_member_of_any_lobby = no
			law_stance = {
				law = law_type:law_free_trade
				value < neutral
			}
		}
		any_political_lobby = {
			is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
			target = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_free_trade
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_member_of_any_lobby = no
				law_stance = {
					law = law_type:law_free_trade
					value < neutral
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_ig
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_ig_leader
			}
		}
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
				target = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_free_trade
				}
			}
			random_lobby_member = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_member # An IG already in the lobby
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			target = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
			}
		}
		currently_enacting_law = {
    		save_scope_as = relevant_law_scope
    	}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:relevant_law_scope }
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby_country }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_events.36.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig
			change_appeasement = {
			   amount = 1
			   factor = appeasement_lobby_pleased
			}
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = ig_unhappy_with_law_enactment
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = lobby_events.36.b
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = small_radicals
			interest_group = scope:relevant_ig
		}
	}
}

# Voting Matters
lobby_events.37 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.37.t
	desc = lobby_events.37.d
	flavor = lobby_events.37.f

	event_image = {
		video = "southamerica_election"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
					}
				}
			}
			AND = {
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_census_voting
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_census_voting
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_census_voting
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
					}
				}
			}
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_ig
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
					}
				}
				target = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			}
		}
		else = {
			limit = {
				is_enacting_law = law_type:law_census_voting
				any_interest_group = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_census_voting
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				any_political_lobby = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_census_voting
					}
				}
			}
			random_interest_group = {
				limit = {
					is_member_of_any_lobby = no
					law_stance = {
						law = law_type:law_census_voting
						value > neutral
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_ig
			}
			random_political_lobby = {
				limit = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					target = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_census_voting
					}
				}
				target = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			}
		}
		currently_enacting_law = {
    		save_scope_as = relevant_law_scope
    	}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { currently_enacting_law = scope:relevant_law_scope }
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby_country }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_events.37.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig
		}
		add_enactment_modifier = {
			name = ig_law_enactment_time_good
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = lobby_events.37.b
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			add_lobby_member = scope:relevant_ig
			change_appeasement = {
			   amount = 1
			   factor = appeasement_lobby_pleased
			}
		}
	}
}

# Ever-Increasing Influence

lobby_events.51 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.51.t
	desc = lobby_events.51.d
	flavor = lobby_events.51.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_octopus"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		has_technology_researched = mass_communication
		any_political_lobby = {
			any_lobby_member = {
				is_powerful = yes
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				any_lobby_member = {
					is_powerful = yes
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			random_lobby_member = {
				limit = {
					is_powerful = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_member
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
	}

	option = { # Chastise a lobby member's actions
		name = lobby_events.51.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby_member = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = lobby_member_approval_lost
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Allow lobby's actions
		name = lobby_events.51.b
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = 1
				factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = lobbyists_unchecked
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
}

	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.52.t
	desc = lobby_events.52.d
	flavor = lobby_events.52.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_clandestine_meeting"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		government_legitimacy < 75
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		any_political_lobby = {
			any_lobby_member = {
				leader = {
					is_ruler = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_political_lobby = {
			random_lobby_member = {
				limit = {
					leader = {
						is_ruler = no
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_member
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			target = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
	}

	option = { # Scrutinize lobby member's actions
		name = lobby_events.52.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby_member = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = lobby_member_pol_str_negative
				days = normal_modifier_time
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = lobbyists_in_check
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
	option = { # Overlook lobby's actions
		name = lobby_events.52.b
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = 1
				factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
			}
		}
	}
}

# Contradictory Correspondence

lobby_events.53 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.53.t
	desc = lobby_events.53.d
	flavor = lobby_events.53.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_printing_press"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		has_technology_researched = mass_communication
		any_political_lobby = {
			OR = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord
			}
			any_lobby_member = {
				leader = {
					is_ruler = no
				}
			}
			target = { relations:root <= relations_threshold:poor }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord
				}
				any_lobby_member = {
					leader = {
						is_ruler = no
					}
				}
				target = {
					relations:root <= relations_threshold:poor
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			random_lobby_member = {
				limit = {
					leader = {
						is_ruler = no
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_member
			}
			target = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
	}

	option = { # Damage is already done
		name = lobby_events.53.a
		default_option = yes
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:relevant_lobby_country
			value = -10
		}
	}
	option = { # Remove from circulation
		name = lobby_events.53.b
		add_loyalists = {
			value = medium_radicals
			interest_group = scope:relevant_lobby_member
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = lobbyists_unchecked
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
}

# Unconventional Guest

lobby_events.54 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.54.t
	desc = lobby_events.54.d
	flavor = lobby_events.54.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_ruler_speaking_to_people"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		any_scope_character = {
			has_role = agitator
			exists = home_country
			NOT = { home_country = ROOT }
			home_country = {
				save_temporary_scope_as = agitator_home_country_temp
			}
		}
		any_political_lobby = {
			OR = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
			}
			target = {
				scope:agitator_home_country_temp ?= THIS
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role = agitator
				exists = home_country
				NOT = { home_country = ROOT }
			}
			home_country = {
				save_scope_as = agitator_home_country
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_agitator
		}
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
				}
				target = {
					scope:agitator_home_country ?= THIS
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		scope:relevant_agitator = {
			is_character_alive = no
		}
	}

	option = { # Discouraged
		name = lobby_events.54.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = -1
				factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Encouraged
		name = lobby_events.54.b
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:agitator_home_country
			value = -10
		}
	}
}

# Problematic Policing

lobby_events.55 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.55.t
	desc = lobby_events.55.d
	flavor = lobby_events.55.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_transfer_of_authority"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_no_police }
		any_political_lobby = {
			OR = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
			}
			any_lobby_member = {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				save_temporary_scope_as = temp_lobby_member
			}
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			is_incorporated = yes
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
				}
				any_lobby_member = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			target = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
			}
			random_lobby_member = {
				limit = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_member
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_incorporated = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_state
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
	}

	option = { # Allow the pro-lobby & police activity to continue
		name = lobby_events.55.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:relevant_lobby_country
			value = 5
		}
	}
	option = { # Condone this
		name = lobby_events.55.b
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = -1
				factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
			}
		}
	}
}

# A Disreputable Bookkeeper

lobby_events.56 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.56.t
	desc = lobby_events.56.d
	flavor = lobby_events.56.f

	event_image = {
		video = "votp_hostile_court"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		government_legitimacy < 75
		any_political_lobby = { # Any lobby type
			any_lobby_member = {
				ig_counts_as_marginal = no
			}
		}
		any_scope_state = { # Government admin building with bureaucrats in capital
			is_capital = yes
			has_building = building_government_administration
			any_scope_pop = {
				is_pop_type = bureaucrats
				pop_employment_building = building_government_administration
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_political_lobby = {
			random_lobby_member = {
				limit = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_member
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_capital = yes
				has_building = building_government_administration
				any_scope_pop = {
					is_pop_type = bureaucrats
					pop_employment_building = building_government_administration
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_state
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
	}

	option = { # Hold lobby accountable
		name = lobby_events.56.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = lobbyists_in_check
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = -1
				factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Write it off as a bookkeeping error
		name = lobby_events.56.b
		scope:relevant_state = {
			add_loyalists_in_state = {
				pop_type = bureaucrats
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
		scope:relevant_lobby_member = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = lobby_member_approval_gained
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

# Advancement in Medicine

lobby_events.57 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.57.t
	desc = lobby_events.57.d
	flavor = lobby_events.57.f

	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_world_fair"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		any_political_lobby = {
			OR = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord
			}
			OR = {
				AND = {
					target = { has_technology_researched = medical_degrees }
					ROOT = {
						NOT = { has_technology_researched = medical_degrees }
						can_research = medical_degrees
					}
				}
				AND = {
					target = { has_technology_researched = pharmaceuticals }
					ROOT = {
						NOT = { has_technology_researched = pharmaceuticals }
						can_research = pharmaceuticals
					}
				}
				AND = {
					target = { has_technology_researched = quinine }
					ROOT = {
						NOT = { has_technology_researched = quinine }
						can_research = quinine
					}
				}
				AND = {
					target = { has_technology_researched = malaria_prevention }
					ROOT = {
						NOT = { has_technology_researched = malaria_prevention }
						can_research = malaria_prevention
					}
				}
				AND = {
					target = { has_technology_researched = antibiotics }
					ROOT = {
						NOT = { has_technology_researched = antibiotics }
						can_research = antibiotics
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						target = { has_technology_researched = medical_degrees }
						ROOT = {
							NOT = { has_technology_researched = medical_degrees }
							can_research = medical_degrees
						}
					}
					AND = {
						target = { has_technology_researched = pharmaceuticals }
						ROOT = {
							NOT = { has_technology_researched = pharmaceuticals }
							can_research = pharmaceuticals
						}
					}
					AND = {
						target = { has_technology_researched = quinine }
						ROOT = {
							NOT = { has_technology_researched = quinine }
							can_research = quinine
						}
					}
					AND = {
						target = { has_technology_researched = malaria_prevention }
						ROOT = {
							NOT = { has_technology_researched = malaria_prevention }
							can_research = malaria_prevention
						}
					}
					AND = {
						target = { has_technology_researched = antibiotics }
						ROOT = {
							NOT = { has_technology_researched = antibiotics }
							can_research = antibiotics
						}
					}
				}
			}
			target = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
	}

	option = { # Build up relationship with relevent_lobby_country
		name = lobby_events.57.a
		default_option = yes
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:relevant_lobby_country
			value = 10
		}
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = 3
				factor = appeasement_lobby_gain
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = lobby_events.57.b
		add_modifier = { # Research tech relevent_lobby_country has
			name = researching_medical_tech_modifer
			multiplier = money_amount_multiplier_small
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
		 if = {
 			limit = {
 				NOT = { has_technology_researched = medical_degrees }
 				can_research = medical_degrees
 			}
 			add_technology_progress = { progress = 2500 technology = medical_degrees }
 		}
 		else_if = {
 			limit = {
 				NOT = { has_technology_researched = pharmaceuticals }
 				can_research = pharmaceuticals
 			}
 			add_technology_progress = { progress = 3500 technology = pharmaceuticals }
 		}
 		 else_if = {
 			limit = {
 				NOT = { has_technology_researched = quinine }
 				can_research = quinine
 			}
 			add_technology_progress = { progress = 3500 technology = quinine }
 		}
 		else_if = {
 			limit = {
 				NOT = { has_technology_researched = malaria_prevention }
 				can_research = malaria_prevention
 			}
 			add_technology_progress = { progress = 5000 technology = malaria_prevention }
 		}
 		else_if = {
 			limit = {
 				NOT = { has_technology_researched = antibiotics }
 				can_research = antibiotics
 			}
 			add_technology_progress = { progress = 5850 technology = antibiotics }
 		}
	}
}

# Gifts Among Friends

lobby_events.58 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = lobby_events.58.t
	desc = lobby_events.58.d
	flavor = lobby_events.58.f

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_power_blocs"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_default.dds"

	duration = 3

	cooldown = { days = very_long_modifier_time }

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_revolutionary = no
		government_legitimacy < 75
		any_scope_state = {
			is_capital = yes
		}
		any_political_lobby = { # Any lobby type
			any_lobby_member = {
				leader = {
					is_ruler = no
					is_busy = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_capital = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_state
		}
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				any_lobby_member = {
					leader = {
						is_ruler = no
						is_busy = no
					}
				}
			}
			random_lobby_member = {
				limit = {
					leader = {
						is_ruler = no
						is_busy = no
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_member
				leader = {
					save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_leader
				}
			}
			target ={
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
			scope:relevant_lobby_leader = {
				is_character_alive = no
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Suggest lobbyists exercise caution in the future
		name = lobby_events.58.a
		default_option = yes
		add_loyalists = {
			value = medium_radicals
			interest_group = scope:relevant_lobby_member
		}
	}
	option = { # Lobby member leader steps down
		name = lobby_events.58.b
		trigger = {
			scope:relevant_lobby_leader ={
				is_immortal = no
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = lobby_member_resigns
			scope:relevant_lobby_leader = {
				hidden_effect = {
					kill_character = { hidden = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = lobbyists_in_check
			days = normal_modifier_time
		}
	}
}

				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
			}
			any_lobby_member = {
				leader = {
					is_ruler = no
				}
				count >= 2
			}
			target = { relations:root <= relations_threshold:poor }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit ={
				OR = {
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
					is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
				}
				any_lobby_member = {
					leader = {
						is_ruler = no
					}
					count >= 2
				}
				target = { relations:root <= relations_threshold:poor }
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			random_lobby_member = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_member
			}
			target = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_lobby_country
			}
		}
	}

	cancellation_trigger = {
		NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_lobby }
	}

	option = { # Let the good press about relevant_lobby_country circulate
		name = lobby_events.60.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:relevant_lobby = {
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = -1
				factor = appeasement_lobby_loss
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = scope:relevant_lobby_country
			value = 10
		}
	}
	option = { # Remove the story from circulation
		name = lobby_events.60.b
		add_loyalists = {
			value = medium_radicals
			interest_group = scope:relevant_lobby_member
		}
	}
}
﻿namespace = lobby_core

lobby_core.1 = { #core event for testing
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3
	orphan = yes

	event_image = {
		video = "middleeast_middleclass_cafe"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"


	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	immediate = {
		random_diplomatically_relevant_country = {
			limit = {
				is_valid_lobby_target = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_country
		}
		random_diplomatically_relevant_country = {
			limit = {
				is_valid_lobby_target = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_country_2
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			save_scope_as = relevant_ig
		}
		random_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					this = scope:relevant_ig
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_ig_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_subject = yes
			}
			random_overlord_or_above = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_country_3
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_core.1.a
		default_option = yes
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				any_political_lobby = {
					exists = this
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = lobby_found_country
			custom_tooltip = lobby_found_tt
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_core.1.b
		create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
			type = catalyst_relations_level_decreased
			target = scope:relevant_country
		}
		create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
			type = catalyst_relations_level_increased
			target = scope:relevant_country_2
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_core.1.c
		create_political_lobby = {
			type = lobby_pro_country
			target = scope:relevant_country
			add_interest_group = scope:relevant_ig
			lobby_formation_reason = diplomacy
		}
		create_political_lobby = {
			type = lobby_anti_country
			target = scope:relevant_country_2
			add_interest_group = scope:relevant_ig_2
			lobby_formation_reason = diplomacy
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = lobby_core.1.d
		highlighted_option = yes
		trigger = {
			is_subject = yes
		}
		create_political_lobby = {
			type = lobby_pro_overlord
			target = scope:relevant_country_3
			add_interest_group = scope:relevant_ig
			lobby_formation_reason = diplomacy
		}
		create_political_lobby = {
			type = lobby_anti_overlord
			target = scope:relevant_country_3
			add_interest_group = scope:relevant_ig_2
			lobby_formation_reason = diplomacy
		}
	}
}

namespace = lobby_proposal

lobby_proposal.1 = { # Pro-Lobby Demand
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	title = lobby_proposal.1.t
	desc = lobby_proposal.1.d
	flavor = lobby_proposal.1.f

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_power_blocs"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"

	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_player = yes
		any_political_lobby = {
			is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
			target = {
				save_temporary_scope_as = relevant_country
			}
		}
		NOR = {
			has_journal_entry = je_anti_lobby_opportunity
			has_journal_entry = je_pro_lobby_opportunity
			has_journal_entry = je_anti_lobby_demand
			has_journal_entry = je_pro_lobby_demand
		}
		any_diplomatically_relevant_country = {
			NOT = { THIS = ROOT }
			has_diplomatic_relevance = ROOT
			"relations(scope:relevant_country)" <= relations_threshold:poor
			OR = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = scope:relevant_country
					type = rivalry
				}
				has_attitude = {
					who = scope:relevant_country
					attitude = belligerent
				}
				has_attitude = {
					who = scope:relevant_country
					attitude = domineering
				}
				has_attitude = {
					who = scope:relevant_country
					attitude = antagonistic
				}
			}
			save_temporary_scope_as = third_party_country
		}
		any_pro_lobby_demand_is_valid = yes
	}



	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:relevant_country
			}
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:third_party_country
			}
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_variable = pro_lobby_proposal_break_alliance
					has_variable = pro_lobby_proposal_break_defensive_pact
					has_variable = pro_lobby_proposal_revoke_trade_privilege
					has_variable = pro_lobby_proposal_embargo
					has_variable = pro_lobby_proposal_rivalry
					has_variable = pro_lobby_proposal_war
					has_variable = pro_lobby_proposal_leave_power_bloc
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			target = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_country
			}
		}
		random_diplomatically_relevant_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { THIS = ROOT }
				has_diplomatic_relevance = ROOT
				"relations(scope:relevant_country)" <= relations_threshold:poor
				OR = {
					has_diplomatic_pact = {
						who = scope:relevant_country
						type = rivalry
					}
					has_attitude = {
						who = scope:relevant_country
						attitude = belligerent
					}
					has_attitude = {
						who = scope:relevant_country
						attitude = domineering
					}
					has_attitude = {
						who = scope:relevant_country
						attitude = antagonistic
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = third_party_country
			ROOT = {
				set_variable = { name = pro_lobby_third_party_var value = PREV days = 180 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:third_party_country
			}
			generate_pro_lobby_diplomatic_pact_proposal = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_proposal.1.a
		default_option = yes
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_pro_lobby_demand
			target = scope:relevant_country
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_proposal.1.b
		scope:relevant_lobby ?= {
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = -5
				factor = appeasement_lobby_request_failed
			}
		}
		clear_pro_lobby_vars = yes
	}
}

lobby_proposal.3 = { # Pro-Lobby Opportunity
	type = country_event
	placement = root

	title = lobby_proposal.3.t
	desc = lobby_proposal.3.d
	flavor = lobby_proposal.3.f

	dlc = dlc010

	duration = 3

	event_image = {
		video = "europenorthamerica_capitalists_meeting"
	}

	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"


	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"

	trigger = {
		has_dlc_feature = lobbies
		is_player = yes
		any_political_lobby = {
			is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
			any_lobby_member = {
				is_in_government = yes
			}
			target = {
				NOR = {
					has_diplomatic_pact = {
						who = ROOT
						type = rivalry
					}
					has_attitude = {
						who = ROOT
						attitude = belligerent
					}
					has_attitude = {
						who = ROOT
						attitude = domineering
					}
					has_attitude = {
						who = ROOT
						attitude = antagonistic
					}
				}
				ROOT = {
					OR = {
						can_create_treaty = {
							first_country = root
							second_country = PREV
							is_draft = no
							binding_period = { years = 2 }
							articles_to_create = {
								{
									article = alliance
								}
							}
						}
						can_create_treaty = {
							first_country = root
							second_country = PREV
							is_draft = no
							binding_period = { years = 2 }
							articles_to_create = {
								{
									article = defensive_pact
								}
							}
						}
						can_create_treaty = {
							first_country = root
							second_country = PREV
							is_draft = no
							binding_period = { years = 2 }
							articles_to_create = {
								{
									article = trade_privilege
									source_country = root
									target_country = PREV
								}
							}
						}
						can_create_diplomatic_pact = { target = PREV type = join_power_bloc }
						can_create_diplomatic_pact = { target = PREV type = invite_to_power_bloc }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		NOR = {
			has_journal_entry = je_anti_lobby_opportunity
			has_journal_entry = je_pro_lobby_opportunity
			has_journal_entry = je_anti_lobby_demand
			has_journal_entry = je_pro_lobby_demand
		}
	}

	cooldown = { days = stupidly_long_modifier_time }

	cancellation_trigger = {
		OR = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:relevant_country
			}
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_variable = pro_lobby_proposal_defensive_pact
					has_variable = pro_lobby_proposal_alliance
					has_variable = pro_lobby_proposal_trade_privilege
					has_variable = pro_lobby_proposal_power_bloc
					has_variable = pro_lobby_proposal_power_bloc_invite
					has_variable = pro_lobby_proposal_investment_agreement
					has_variable = pro_lobby_proposal_military_assistance
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
				any_lobby_member = {
					is_in_government = yes
				}
				target = {
					NOR = {
						has_diplomatic_pact = {
							who = ROOT
							type = rivalry
						}
						has_attitude = {
							who = ROOT
							attitude = belligerent
						}
						has_attitude = {
							who = ROOT
							attitude = domineering
						}
						has_attitude = {
							who = ROOT
							attitude = antagonistic
						}
					}
					ROOT = {
						OR = {
							can_create_treaty = {
								first_country = root
								second_country = PREV
								is_draft = no
								binding_period = { years = 2 }
								articles_to_create = {
									{
										article = alliance
									}
								}
							}
							can_create_treaty = {
								first_country = root
								second_country = PREV
								is_draft = no
								binding_period = { years = 2 }
								articles_to_create = {
									{
										article = defensive_pact
									}
								}
							}
							can_create_treaty = {
								first_country = root
								second_country = PREV
								is_draft = no
								binding_period = { years = 2 }
								articles_to_create = {
									{
										article = trade_privilege
										source_country = root
										target_country = PREV
									}
								}
							}
							can_create_diplomatic_pact = { target = PREV type = join_power_bloc }
							can_create_diplomatic_pact = { target = PREV type = invite_to_power_bloc }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_lobby
			target = {
				save_scope_as = relevant_country
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:relevant_country
			}
			generate_pro_lobby_diplomatic_pact_opportunity = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_proposal.3.a
		default_option = yes
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_pro_lobby_opportunity
			target = scope:relevant_country
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = lobby_proposal.3.b
		clear_pro_lobby_vars = yes
	}
}
